# Sovereign Platinum — development status

_Last updated: 2026-10-22, 2:5x pm US Central (handoff point the owner asked for). Branch `claude/sweet-wright-rk4wl1`,
reset to main `5b427e88` after PR #14 merged. Everything below is on main; nothing is uncommitted or in progress._

## 1. What was just implemented

**PR #14 merged: E6 is complete. All 1025 species are in the game.**
- 532 new species, generated by `tools/engine/gen_new_species.py`.
- Placeholder sprites; no cries for the new species (owner: cries come last).
- English-only Pokédex.
- Easy Chat species words, from `tools/engine/gen_easy_chat_species.py`.
  - The Easy Chat word list now lives in the `easy_chat` overlay (owner request), which frees 9,504 bytes of RAM
    that was always in use.
- Test build: SELECT+B opens Easy Chat.
  - The task lives in `script_manager.c`, because overlay5 is unloaded while Easy Chat runs.
  - Lesson: any field task that opens an app must live outside overlay5.
- Verified in the emulator and by CI (ROM build, boot smoke test, docs check).

**Owner decisions recorded this session** (`docs/ProjectStatus.md` decisions 15–18, `CLAUDE.md`, roadmap):
- **15: Zacian, Zamazenta and Ogerpon items.**
  - Wild Zacian and Zamazenta hold the Rusted Sword and Rusted Shield.
  - Ogerpon holds the Teal Mask.
  - The Pokéfan (male) in **Celestic Town's northeast house** (`CelesticTownNortheastHouse_PokefanM`) gives the
    Wellspring, Hearthflame and Cornerstone Masks when Ogerpon leads the party.
  - Documented in `docs/ItemChanges_Sovereign.md` ("Form items") and `data/postgame_legendaries.yaml`.
- **16: every Pokémon form is in the game.** Owner rule, also in `CLAUDE.md`.
  - Exceptions: no Dynamax/Gigantamax, and no unplayable forms (Eternamax Eternatus, Koraidon's Builds, Miraidon's
    Modes).
  - Terapagos-Stellar is shelved.
  - That leaves 282 alternate Pokémon plus about 228 cosmetic forms. New milestone **FM** in
    `docs/EngineRoadmap.md`.
- **17: battle-screen button.** A MEGA button goes on the touch-screen move menu (E8). Primal Reversion stays
  automatic on switch-in.
- **18: Totem forms come from 11 Totem trials** along the story, all before the Elite Four, never wild.
  - Placements and levels are approved: `data/totem_trials.yaml`.
  - Each trial is a double battle. The Totem has its aura and calls up to 2 reserve helpers into the empty slot.
  - Balls can be thrown only once the Totem is alone; it is caught in Totem form.
  - Still to fill in: the helper species per trial, and each aura checked against Bulbapedia.

**E7 step 1 done: the 245 new items exist (IDs 468–712).**
- `tools/engine/gen_items.py` writes `decomp/res/items/data/<item>.json` and appends the constants to
  `decomp/generated/items.txt`. Run it again after editing the list; it's idempotent.
- Names, prices, fling power and descriptions come from PokeAPI. The 26 Gen IX items PokeAPI has no English text for
  use descriptions written in `DESCRIPTIONS` in the generator. ASCII ' becomes ’, since Platinum's charmap has no '.
- For now every new item:
  - uses the placeholder "none" icon;
  - has `HOLD_EFFECT_NONE`;
  - has no field use, except evolution items (`ITEM_USE_FUNC_EVO_STONE`, Scrolls included).
- Key items go to the Key Items pocket; everything else goes to Items.
- Names are capped at 16 characters. Three are shortened in `SHORT_NAMES`: Unremark. Teacup, Masterpc. Teacup,
  Darkness Scroll.
- Verified (test build): ROM builds, `boot_smoke.py` passes, a save continues, the bag opens, a wild battle works.
- **Not checked yet:** the new items on screen (none is in the test bag yet).

**E7 design:**
- `data/items_sovereign.yaml` lists the **245 new items**, every name checked against PokeAPI:
  - 38 held items and 18 Gems.
  - Form items: Memories, Drives, Nectars, Sweets, Rusted Sword/Shield, the four masks, the origin
    crystals/orbs, Ultranecrozium Z, Blue/Red Orb, Galarica items, Chipped Pot, Masterpiece Teacup.
  - 14 evolution items, plus the 2 Scrolls (key items). Strawberry Sweet, the 16th, is with Alcremie's sweets.
  - 21 Mints, Ability Capsule and Ability Patch.
  - 10 key items.
  - 92 Mega Stones, taken from `data/megas.csv`.
- Berries (Roseli, Kee, Maranga) are deferred: the berry code assumes one contiguous berry ID block.

## 3. Next 3 steps (E7 items)

1. **Finish E7's item basics.** `gen_items.py` is done; still to do:
   - Give the test bag some new items (e.g. in `SovereignTest_SetupNewGame`, `src/script_manager.c`) and check them
     on screen.
   - Real icons, from PokeAPI item sprites converted to 24×24 / 16 colours, credited in CITATIONS.
   - Mint and Ability Capsule/Patch use functions.
   - The reference notes below are kept for this work.
   - How items work in the decomp:
     - IDs come from `decomp/generated/items.txt`: append before `MAX_ITEMS`, after `ITEM_SECRET_KEY` (#467).
     - Each `ITEM_X` reads `decomp/res/items/data/x.json`. Fields: name, plural, article, description (≤3 lines,
       ≤40 chars each), icon `{sprite, palette}`, price, holdEffect, fieldPocket, fieldUseFunc and so on. Copy the
       shape of `choice_band.json`.
     - `tools/dataproc/src/itemproc.c` packs the data and generates the name/description text banks.
     - Icons are `res/items/icons/<x>.png` + `.pal`, listed in `res/items/icons/meson.build` and
       `res/items/item_icon.order`.
     - Hold effects are listed in `decomp/generated/item_hold_effects.txt`.
     - Evolution items use `ITEM_USE_FUNC_EVO_STONE`; itemproc sets `partyUseParam.evolve` for those.
   - Take prices, descriptions and fling data from PokeAPI's `item_*.csv` files (pinned commit:
     `tools/POKEAPI_COMMIT`).
   - Placeholder icons can come from PokeAPI's item sprites, the way the species icons did. Credit them in
     CITATIONS.
   - New hold effects start as `HOLD_EFFECT_NONE`; their battle effects are a later checklist step.
   - Mints and the Ability Capsule/Patch need new field-use functions (QoL item).
2. **Check item names on screen.** Platinum's longest item name is 12 characters, but many new names are 14–19
   (Heavy-Duty Boots, Ultranecrozium Z, Unremarkable Teacup, and Mega Stones like Charizardite X). The bag's name
   buffer is 18 characters (`src/applications/bag/main.c:1009`). Test what fits in the bag list, the shop list and
   the battle bag, then add a short-name table like `SHORT_NAMES` in `gen_new_species.py` for anything that
   overflows. Also check bag pocket capacities against the new items.
3. **Wire up the 16 waiting evolutions** (rerun `gen_new_species.py` once the items exist), then the shops.
   - New held items go on sale at the Veilstone Department Store (owner).
   - Evolution items: Renegade's Snowpoint seller.
   - Mega Stone vendors: `docs/MegaEvolution.md`.
   - After E7: held-item forms (Crowned Zacian/Zamazenta, Ogerpon masks) under milestone FM, then E8 (Mega
     Evolution).

**Also pending:** a read-only survey of the decomp's item system (limits, bit widths, bag sizes, shop data) was
running in a subagent at handoff. If it's lost, redo it before writing `gen_items.py`.

**Testing tips:**
- Test ROM: `tools/test/build_test_rom.sh` → `decomp/build_test/pokeplatinum.us.nds`.
  - A cold build takes about 40 minutes on 4 cores. Install the apt packages from `decomp/INSTALL.md`, then
    `pip install py-desmume pillow pyyaml openpyxl`.
- The docs CI job runs `tools/regenerate_docs.sh /tmp/pokeapi/data/v2/csv decomp` and fails on any diff in `data/`
  or `docs/`.
  - Generated docs (`MegaEvolution.md`, `PostgameLegendaries.md` and so on) must be edited through their generators
    in `tools/design/`.
  - Fetch PokeAPI at the pinned commit as the workflow does.
- Booting a battery save is more reliable than `new_game.sh`, whose intro timing shifts between builds. Make an
  in-game save (menu → SAVE → YES, YES, wait about 1,800 frames, then `export:`). Boot it with `drive.py ... sav:FILE`
  and continue with `wait:600 A wait:200 START wait:200 A wait:300`.
- `drive.py`'s `PLAYER_X/Z_ADDR` read (0,0) on current builds and need re-finding.
- Easy Chat (test build): `SELECT+B`, then `A A` opens the POKéMON group.

## CB-0001 — 2026-10-22, 2:46 pm US Central (session on `claude/sweet-wright-rk4wl1`)

From now on this file is append-only (owner rule, recorded in `CLAUDE.md`). Everything above this line was written
by this session before the rule. This entry updates it without rewriting it.

**Implemented since the text above:**
- Found why the new items seemed not to reach the bag: **the test harness, not the game.**
  - py-desmume keeps the battery in `~/.config/desmume/pokeplatinum.us.dsv` and loads it on every boot.
  - So `drive.py ... -` (a "fresh" boot) and `tools/test/new_game.sh` CONTINUED the in-game save made earlier on the
    E6 build, and the new-game test setup never ran.
  - Checked: the new item data packs correctly (`pl_item_data.narc` has 691 members; Eviolite decodes as price 4000,
    fling power 40, Items pocket). The ID map is right, and `SovereignTest_SetupNewGame` does call `Bag_TryAddItem`
    with the new IDs.
- `tools/test/drive.py` now boots with a blank battery when no state or battery is given, so a fresh boot is really
  fresh.
- Test build: the new-game bag gets six E7 items (Assault Vest, Heavy-Duty Boots, Unremark. Teacup, Charizardite X,
  Metal Alloy, Mega Ring) for on-screen checks.

**Files modified:**
- `tools/test/drive.py` (blank battery on a fresh boot)
- `decomp/src/script_manager.c` (E7 items in the test bag)
- `CLAUDE.md` (append-only status rule)
- `CLAUDE_STATUS.md` (this entry)

**Next 3 steps:**
1. Fix `tools/test/new_game.sh` for a truly blank battery. It now stalls at the intro's touch-screen Yes/No prompt

## CB-0003 — 2026-10-22, 2:58 pm US Central (session on `claude/sweet-wright-rk4wl1`)

**Implemented:**
- **Bag icons for all 245 new items.** `tools/engine/gen_items.py /path/to/pokeapi/csv /path/to/PokeAPI/sprites`
  converts PokeAPI's `sprites/items/<item>.png` to Platinum's icon format.
  - Format: 32×32, **4-bit** PNG, index 0 transparent, 15 colours, big renders scaled to 24 px. The `.pal` is
    JASC with CRLF line endings.
  - Each icon is listed in generator-owned marker blocks in `decomp/res/items/icons/meson.build` (one block per
    list) and appended to `decomp/res/items/item_icon.order` after `unused_710.NCLR`.
  - Three sprite names differ from the item names: N-Solarizer, N-Lunarizer, Ultranecrozium Z (`SPRITE_NAMES`).
  - Lesson: an 8-bit indexed PNG gets packed as garbage tiles. Save with `bits=4`.
- Verified in the test build: all six test-bag items show their icons in the bag, and the boot smoke test passes.
- CITATIONS: new "Item icons" section (PokeAPI sprites commit `97a7832`).

- `decomp/res/items/data/*.json` (icon fields)
- `CITATIONS.md`, `docs/EngineChangelog.md`
- `CLAUDE_STATUS.md` (this entry)

  `PartyMenu_TryUseSovereignTrainingItem` (`decomp/src/applications/party_menu/main.c`).
  - Data: the 6 spare bits at block B 0x19 now hold `mintNature` (5 bits, nature+1) and `abilitySlotSwapped`
    (1 bit). Accessors: `MON_DATA_MINT_NATURE` and `MON_DATA_ABILITY_SLOT_SWAPPED`, appended to
    `decomp/generated/pokemon_data_params.txt`. `MON_DATA_UNUSED_113` now reads 0 and ignores writes.
  - `BoxPokemon_GetNature` returns the Mint nature (stats, summary). Breeding, Synchronize and flavours keep the
    personality's nature.
  - `BoxPokemon_CalcAbility` XORs the personality's slot bit with `abilitySlotSwapped`, so the Capsule survives
    evolution.
  - The Patch toggles the Hidden Ability flag (Scarlet/Violet rules). The Capsule does nothing with a Hidden Ability
    or a single regular ability.
  - New party-menu texts: `PartyMenu_Text_SovereignMintUsed` and `PartyMenu_Text_SovereignAbilityChanged`.

  dex number never shows "???". Open question in `ProjectStatus.md`: expand the Sinnoh regional dex, show

## CA-0002 — 2026-10-22, 3:21 pm US Central (session on `claude/festive-bardeen-5h7beo`)

**Implemented: milestone E9, the Sovereign trainer format.** Reference: `docs/TrainerFormat.md`.
- **New optional fields** on each trainer party member: `nature`, `ability`, `ivs`, `evs`, `gender`, `shiny` and
  `ball`.
  - Any one of them switches the party to the new 38-byte layout `TrainerMonSovereign` (`TRDATATYPE_SOVEREIGN`),
    which also stores the whole species ID.
  - Platinum's 928 trainers pack byte-for-byte as before.
- **How the game builds each Pokémon:**
  - It takes Platinum's seeded personality value and changes it as little as possible to get the nature, gender and
    ability slot.
  - Hidden Abilities use Sovereign's flag; shininess comes from the OT ID.
  - IVs, EVs and form are set, then the ability and stats are recalculated.
- **Test trainer `TRAINER_SOVEREIGN_TEST`:** the developer team at Lv. 100, as a double battle. The test build's
  SELECT+R battles it now (it was the Route 209 rival).
  - It took the unused `FLAG_UNK_0x08F0` as its defeated flag.
  - "Adding a trainer" steps are in `TrainerFormat.md`.
- **New tool `tools/test/check_trainer_party.py ROM XMAP SAVE [JSON]`:** decrypts the opponent's Pokémon in game and
  compares them with the JSON, stats included.
- **Verified:**
  - The checker passes for all six Pokémon (two Hidden Abilities, two shinies, an Ultra Ball, every nature, IV and EV

- The biggest problem: only Earthquake and Magnitude are checked, by name, for hitting the AI's own partner. Surf,
  Discharge, Lava Plume, Explosion and the Gen V+ ally-hitting moves aren't.

## CA-0010 — 2026-10-22, 4:22 pm US Central (session on `claude/festive-bardeen-5h7beo`)

**Implemented:**
- **12b #1–#2, partner safety in doubles.**
  - The new AI command `LoadPartnerDamagePercent` estimates how much of the partner's HP any move aimed at every
    adjacent Pokémon would take, using the game's damage calc. Immunities come from the type chart and from
    Platinum's absorbing abilities.
  - `TagStrategy_CheckSpecialScoring` scores it: +2 unhurt, −10 KO, −5 for ≥50%, −3 for ≥25%, −1 otherwise.
  - Platinum's by-name handlers for Earthquake, Magnitude, Discharge, Lava Plume and Surf are removed.
- **Verified in game** with the new `tools/test/log_ai_partner_damage.py`: Earthquake reads 0% next to Charizard
  and Surf 0% next to a Water Absorb Vaporeon, and the other hits scale as expected. A doubles turn plays, the trainer
  checker passes, and `boot_smoke.py` passes.
  - Bug found and fixed while testing: `TrainerAI_MoveType` returns Normal for ordinary moves (it only handles
    variable-type ones), so the absorb check uses the move's own type.
- **Story:** a Totem-trial prompts-and-gate proposal in `docs/Story.md` (owner request).
  - Gate: the Sovereign battle needs all 11 trials; the main story stays ungated.
  - Prompts: a Sovereign Seal progress item, Pokémon Center rumours, Town Map marks, and Cynthia pointing out unlit
    gems.
  - Waiting on the owner's answer.

  (Eterna) needs Ribombee and Vikavolt; Cyrus (Celestic) needs Mimikyu, Marowak, Raticate, Lurantis and Araquanid; the
  lakes need Salazzle; Spear Pillar needs Kommo-o. Togedemaru gates nothing and is always last. Decision 18 updated:
  the trials are no longer optional.
- **Crown.** After each trial's catch encounter (caught or not), the player finds a crown piece: Gold Shard first,
  then jewels or gold shards, and Togedemaru's is the Crown Diamond. All are key items, so they can't be sold or
  tossed. Henry appears at Spear Pillar only with all 11.
- **The Celestic elder** meets the player at home after the first Hall of Fame and sends them to Spear Pillar.
- **The Elite Four after the Sovereign battle:** rematches continue, all Lv. 100 (a third set of teams, T1).

**Files modified:** `docs/Story.md`, `docs/ProjectStatus.md` (decisions 18 and 23), `docs/DeveloperBattle.md`,
`data/totem_trials.yaml` (header comment), `CLAUDE_STATUS.md`.

  #19 had a conflict, which is why its CI hadn't run.
- **12b #3, spread credit.** New AI command `LoadOtherFoeDamagePercent` estimates a spread move's hit on the foe it
  isn't scored against. `TagStrategy_CheckOtherFoe` in `script.s` scores that hit: +2 if it's a knockout, +1 if it
  takes ≥25% of the foe's HP, −2 if the foe absorbs the type.
  - It shares one damage estimate with `LoadPartnerDamagePercent` (`AI_SpreadDamagePercent`, `AI_MoveDealsDamage`).
  - Verified in the test build: Heat Wave reads a KO on the other foe; with a Flash Fire Heatran (temporary test
    party swap, reverted) it reads "absorbs"; single-target moves read "not hit".
  - The partner check reads as before. ROM and test ROM build.

## CB-0019 — 2026-10-22, 8:20 pm US Central (session on `claude/sweet-wright-rk4wl1`)

**Implemented: the Mega Evolution and Primal Reversion animation** (E8 step 4, `docs/MegaEvolution.md`), verified frame
by frame in the test build:
- The background darkens and the other battlers dim.
- Light beams and gathering sparkles reach the Pokémon, and a ring of light forms around it.
- It pulses pink, cyan and gold (blues for Kyogre, reds for Groudon) as it shakes, then turns white.
- The sprite swaps while it's white. Streaks burst out, the new form fades in from white with sparkles, and it cries.
- About 6 seconds in all. It was checked on Garchomp (wild), Kyogre and Groudon (temporary leads), and the foe's

## CA-0022 — 2026-10-23 (session on `claude/festive-bardeen-5h7beo`)

**Implemented:** 12b #8 checked; no code change needed now. `docs/AIReview.md` has a new *#8* section:
- **How the AI scores partner-aimed moves:** −30 for any damaging move unless the partner absorbs it, and −30 for
  any status move Platinum doesn't name. A partner target needs a score of 100 or more to be used, so the AI never
  attacks or wrongly buffs its partner.
- **What the Gen V+ ally moves do now:** E3 maps them to Gen IV effects that act on the user, e.g. Heal Pulse acts
  like Recover and Decorate like Swords Dance, so none can hurt the AI's side.
- **Item 11:** each move gets partner scoring as it gets its exact effect, per the table (Pollen Puff and Heal Pulse
  heal a hurt partner, Instruct, After You, and so on).
- Also merged main (CB-0016 to CB-0018).

**Files modified:** `docs/AIReview.md`, `CLAUDE_STATUS.md`.

## CB-0028 — 2026-10-23, 1:44 am US Central (session on `claude/sweet-wright-rk4wl1`)

**Implemented:** the first Totem trial on the map, and the crown pieces (decisions 18 and 23). PR #35 (the Totem forms
and trial battle) is merged.
- **Totems on the map.** All 11 Totems have a huge 64x64 overworld sprite (Regigigas's setup), made from their Totem
  form's sprite with an orange aura outline (`tools/sprites/make_totem_overworld.py`, `OBJ_EVENT_GFX_TOTEM_*`).
- **Totem Gumshoos** stands on Route 205 South in the flower bed by the river (x 197, z 656). The north bank, where
  the Yungoos grass is, is past the Galactic grunts, so the Totem stands on the near side.
- **One trial script for all 11** (`CommonScript_SovereignTotemTrial`, `Common_SovereignTotemTrial`): the map script
  only sets `VAR_0x8004` to the trial.
  - Talking to the Totem plays its cry, then "A huge GUMSHOOS glares at you!", then the battle.
  - It needs two Pokémon that can battle until 1 vs 2 exists.
  - **Win or catch:** the trial flag is set, and the Totem leaves its crown piece ("Something is glinting on the
    ground...", "A found a Gold Shard!").
  - **Lose:** a blackout, and the Totem is back next time.
  - The map's OnLoad removes the Totem as the field comes back after the battle, like Regigigas.
- **Flags:** `FLAG_SOVEREIGN_TOTEM_TRIAL_*` (the unused 0x167–0x171), in trial order. Each is also its Totem's hidden
  flag, for the Galactic gates to check.
- **New script commands:** `SovereignGetTotemTrialSpecies` and `SovereignClearFlagFromVar`.
- **The crown:** 13 new key items (11 pieces, the Crown, the Sovereign Crown) in `data/items_sovereign.yaml`.
  - Their descriptions hint more and more that the pieces fit together.
  - With all 11 held, they merge into the Crown.
  - The icons are placeholders drawn by `tools/sprites/make_crown_icons.py`; `gen_items.py` takes `icon: drawn`.
- **Test build:** SELECT+L+R's 9th stop is 3 steps south of Totem Gumshoos.
- **`tools/test/find_tiles.py`** now marks every water tile the engine lets you Surf on. It used to know only the
  river behaviour and showed Route 205 South's sea-type river as walkable.
- **Verified:**
  - The talk, the battle and the win; "disappeared from view"; the Gold Shard in the Key Items pocket with its icon
    and description.
  - The Totem stays gone after leaving and coming back.
  - The merge, in a temporary build that started with the other 10 pieces: the Crown obtained, all 11 pieces gone.
  - The loss branch, in a temporary build that sent every result there: a blackout home, and the Totem back.
  - The release ROM builds without warnings and passes boot smoke; the docs regenerate clean.
- **Watch:** the Key Items pocket holds 50 (`KEY_ITEM_POCKET_SIZE`). Platinum has 40 key items (several only held for a
  while) and Sovereign adds 26. Up to 10 pieces are held before the merge, so a full late-game bag could get close.
  If it does, raise the pocket size (a save-layout change).

  - With the Chronicle open and a party Pokémon that knows Sing (`FindPartySlotWithMove`), he asks if it will sing the
    Relic Song. On yes, its cry plays and `FLAG_SOVEREIGN_MELOETTA_SONG` (the former `FLAG_UNUSED_0x02CD`) is set.
  - Meloetta then stands on the Contest Hall's stage (28, 5) until it's caught.
  - **Moved from Amity Square** (the yaml said its stage). Amity Square has no stage the player can reach (its raised
    sandy area is walled in), and only lets in players leading certain Pokémon.
  - It comes in its Aria Forme; the Pirouette Forme waits for the battle forms (FM).
- **Genesect** (Lv. 60):
  - With the Chronicle open, the Galactic HQ lab's male scientist confesses that the lab revived and remade a fossil
    Pokémon. That sets `FLAG_SOVEREIGN_GENESECT_FREED` (the former `FLAG_UNUSED_0x02CE`), and `AddObject` shows
    Genesect in the back room (34, 5), by the Control Room door.
  - It stays there until it's caught.
  - Its Drive forms wait for FM.
- **The Archivist:** after the swords and Keldeo, he gives a hint for each of Meloetta and Genesect while it isn't
  caught. Keldeo's two lines now end with a page break, since more can follow.
- **Sprites:** `OBJ_EVENT_GFX_LEGENDARY_MELOETTA` and `_GENESECT` (`sovlegend05`, `sovlegend06`), from
  `make_legendary_overworld.py`. The other four sprites come out byte for byte the same. `CITATIONS.md` lists ids 648
  and 649.
- **Verified** (test build with a temporary patch: game completed, Chronicle open, Master Balls, party at level 100,
  Starly taught Sing at one warp, the scientist kept in place, test warps; all removed before committing):
  - the Archivist's new hints, and his talk without them once both were caught;
  - the composer without Sing, then the question with Sing, answering no and then yes;
  - Meloetta hidden before the song, shown after, caught, and the stage empty afterwards;
  - Genesect hidden before the confession, shown after, caught, and the room empty afterwards;
  - the scientist's and the composer's lines once their Pokémon is caught.
  - The release ROM builds without warnings and passes boot smoke; the docs regenerate clean.

  - when it switches in (checked once per switch-in, through the new `sovereignSchoolingChecked` battle-mon bit);

  the turn after it drops to 1/4 HP, not mid-turn. Release build without warnings, boot smoke, docs regenerated.

## CB-0054 — Fix: Toxic's battle script overflowed into the player's Pokémon (Fri Oct 23 2026, 23:00 CDT)

**Implemented:**
- **Bug found while testing Shields Down:** after a Toxic, the player's lead had "no moves left!" and could only
  Struggle. `subscript_badly_poison` was 1620 bytes and the battle script buffer holds 1600
  (`BATTLE_SCRIPT_SIZE_MAX` 400 u32s). The buffer sits right before `battleMons[0]`, so loading the script overwrote
  battler 0's species, stats and moves. A DeSmuME write watch on the battle mon caught it.
- `BATTLE_SCRIPT_SIZE_MAX` is now 512, and `BattleSystem_CallScript`'s assert uses the constant instead of `400 * 4`.
- New `tools/test/check_battle_scripts.py`: fails when a built battle script doesn't fit. CI runs it after the build.
- **Verified:** after the fix a Toxic on a wild Zangoose poisoned it through Mold Breaker (Immunity then cured it), and
  the next turn's moves worked. Release build without warnings, boot smoke, docs.

**Files modified:** `decomp/include/constants/battle.h`, `decomp/src/battle/battle_lib.c`,
`tools/test/check_battle_scripts.py` (new), `.github/workflows/build.yml`, `docs/TechnicalDesign.md`,
`docs/EngineChangelog.md`, `CLAUDE_STATUS.md`.

**Next 3 steps:**
1. Finish milestone FM's Minior (Meteor colors from personality, Shields Down) on top of this fix; it is built and
   tested on a local branch.
2. Milestone FM: Power Construct (Complete Zygarde) and Battle Bond (Ash-Greninja).
3. Owner decisions still open: Revival Blessing; Koraidon/Miraidon gate; Cosmog's Teleport; whether Ultra Burst
   counts as the trainer's Mega; overworld sprites and sizing; final placements.

**Implemented:**
- **Zygarde's forms** in `gen_forms.py`, in the official order:
  - 1 = 10%, 2 = 10% with Power Construct, 3 = 50% with Power Construct. These are `SOVEREIGN_FORM_KEY_ITEM` with the

  - 4 and 5 = Complete, from forms 2 and 3. These are `SOVEREIGN_FORM_BATTLE` with Power Construct; 5 is an alias of

    Reveal Glass and Prison Bottle still toggle on and off.

    and Destruction.

    `subscript_sovereign_power_construct` (new; added to `sub_seq.order`, `meson.build` and
    `generated/battle_subscripts.txt`).

## CB-0057 — Hangry Morpeko (Hunger Switch) and Aura Wheel (Fri Oct 23 2026, 23:47 CDT)

**Implemented:**
- `"morpeko-hangry": (KIND_BATTLE, "ABILITY_HUNGER_SWITCH")` in `gen_forms.py` (form 1).
- **Hunger Switch:** a `BattleSystem_TriggerTurnEndAbility` case switches Morpeko between Full Belly and Hangry at the
  end of each turn, with `subscript_form_change`. The usual battle-form reverts happen on switch-out and at the end of
  battle.
- **Aura Wheel:**
  - It now uses `SOVEREIGN_RAISE_SPEED_HIT`, 100% Speed +1 (`gen9_move_fixes.py`).
  - `Sovereign_AuraWheelType` gives Dark-type for a Hangry Morpeko, else Electric. The damage calc, `CalcMoveType`
    and `Move_CalcVariableType` all use it.
  - Not built: in the official games, Aura Wheel fails for other users (Sketch, Mimic).
- **Ability checklist:** `gen_abilities.py` `IMPLEMENTED` now lists the form abilities built: Zen Mode, Stance
  Change, Shields Down, Schooling, Disguise, Power Construct and Hunger Switch.
- **Verified** in the emulator: Hangry, then Full Belly, then Hangry over three turns; then a Dark Aura Wheel was
  super effective on Gastly, with Speed +1.

## CB-0060 — Battle Bond Greninja gift and Ash-Greninja (Sat Oct 24 2026, 00:25 CDT)

**Implemented:**

  Greninja is now form 3 (Megas are never saved).
- **Battle Bond:**
  - It is a case in `AFTER_MOVE_HIT_STATE_KNOCKOUT_ABILITY` (`battle_controller_player.c`), using the new
    `subscript_sovereign_battle_bond` and 6 battle strings.
  - Switching out doesn't revert it (`Pokemon_SovereignRevertSwitchOutForm`).
  - Ash-Greninja's Water Shuriken: `Sovereign_IsAshGreninja`; 20 power in `Sovereign_MovePower`, 3 hits in
    `BtlCmd_SetMultiHit`.
- **The gift NPC:**
  - An Ace Trainer in the Fight Area Pokémon Center 1F at (12, 6). It's the Battle Frontier's town; the Frontier has
    no Pokémon Center of its own.
  - Level 50, the demo's moves, once (`FLAG_SOVEREIGN_RECEIVED_BATTLE_BOND_GRENINJA` replaces `FLAG_UNUSED_0x0101`).
  - Uses a new script command, `SovereignSetPartyMonForm partySlot, form` (`scrcmd.c`, `scrcmd.h`, `scrcmd.inc`).
- The ability checklist lists Battle Bond.

  `Error 1`.

  scientist (future Paradox) and a fossil maniac (ancient Paradox).

    each. Cyclizar is excluded.
  - Koraidon's encounter opens after all the ancient ones are obtained; Miraidon's after all the future ones.
- **Cosmog:** remove Teleport from its moveset.

## CB-0063 — FM: Ice Face and Gulp Missile (Sat Oct 24 2026, 01:21 CDT)

**Implemented:**
- **Ice Face (Noice Eiscue).**
  - The first physical hit on Eiscue does no damage and makes it Noice Face, which lasts through switching until the
    battle ends.
  - Snow (Platinum's hail) brings the ice back when Eiscue switches in during it or when snow starts, once per snow.
- **Gulp Missile (Gulping and Gorging Cramorant).**
  - Surf (when it hits) or Dive (its first turn) catches an Arrokuda above half HP, or a Pikachu at half or less.
  - A damaging hit spits the catch: 1/4 of the attacker's max HP (Magic Guard prevents it), then Defense -1
    (Arrokuda) or paralysis (Pikachu).
  - Not while Cramorant is underwater. The catch is lost on a switch-out.
- Verified in the emulator: Ice Face's block, Noice through a switch, snow restoring the ice once; Gorging (Surf at
  2/5 HP) spitting a paralyzing Pikachu; Gulping (Dive) spitting an Arrokuda for Defense -1.

## CB-0064 — FM: Terastal Terapagos (Sat Oct 24 2026, 01:43 CDT)

**Implemented:**
- **Tera Shift:** Terapagos becomes Terastal as it enters battle. Its HP rises with its max HP (as in Scarlet and
  Violet). It stays Terastal through switching, until the battle ends.
- **Tera Shell:** at full HP, a damaging move it isn't immune to is not very effective, for every hit of a multi-hit
  move. Mold Breaker ignores it. A Terastal Terapagos switching back in keeps Tera Shell.
- Verified in the emulator: the transformation and HP rise on entry, Tera Shell in the summary, Terastal after a
  switch, and Tackle "not very effective" at full HP.
- With this, every battle form in `data/forms.csv` is in except the shelved Stellar Form.

## CA-0054 (Claude A, 2026-10-24): scripted sales half price after the Hall of Fame; trainer rework log

**Implemented:**
- **Decision 43:** after the Hall of Fame, the scripted sales are half price: honey (Floaroma Meadow), Moomoo Milk (Café
  Cabin), the 5F vending machines and the B1F Lava Cookies and Poffins (Veilstone Dept. Store), Mulch (Route 208 house),
  the Day Care rancher's EV berry bundle and Game Corner coins. Each price check and charge has a `...SovereignHalf`
  twin reached with `GoToIfSet FLAG_GAME_COMPLETED`; the price lines and menu entries have half-price variants
  (`...SovereignHalf` texts, `MenuEntries_Text_Sovereign...Half`). Generated by a one-off script.
- **No resale profit:** `Shop_IsItemSoldInMarts` (`src/scrcmd_shop.c`) now also lists the items those NPCs sell, so
  after the Hall of Fame they sell for half their value, which is what they cost. Every item's value equals its old
  sale price, so buying and selling back breaks even, for these and for every Poké Mart item (e.g. an Antidote: bought
  for ₽50, sold for ₽50).
- **Decision 42:** every trainer team gets reworked. `docs/TrainerTeamLog.md` lists all 1,015 trainer files (class, name,
  party size, format, map) with a status column; all are "Not started", and the boss rows note their T1 drafts.
- Release build and boot smoke test pass. Not tested in game: the half-price branches.

pokemon_day_care,game_corner,menu_entries}.json`, `decomp/src/scrcmd_shop.c`, `docs/TrainerTeamLog.md` (new),

## CB-0104 — 2026-10-24, 5:10 pm US Central (session on `claude/sweet-wright-rk4wl1`)

**Implemented: CA-0055's request, decision 43's half-price scripted sales tested in game, and a bug fixed.** PR #119
(part 29, Lugia and Ho-Oh) is merged.
- **For Claude A — bug in the half-price menus, fixed:** after the Hall of Fame, the vending machines, the Route 208
  Mulch shop and the Game Corner coin clerk showed blank entries; only "NO THANKS" or "EXIT" was readable.
  - Cause: `AddMenuEntryImm` stores its text ID in one byte. `menu_entries.json` has 292 entries now, so the nine
    `MenuEntries_Text_Sovereign...Half` entries (283–291) were cut to their low byte.
  - They now use `AddMenuEntry` (a halfword ID). The charges were already right.
  - Watch for this when adding menu entries: anything past index 255 needs `AddMenuEntry`.
- **New CI check:** `tools/test/check_byte_text_ids.py` (rom job). It reports `Message`-type and `AddMenuEntryImm`
  texts past 255. Every map text archive is under 256 messages today.
- **Results on post-game and pre-game test saves:**
  - Post-game, every price line, menu and charge matched: Moomoo Milk ₽250 and ₽3,000 a dozen; honey ₽50 and ₽500 for
    10; vending ₽100/150/175 (Fresh Water bought); Lava Cookies ₽100 and ₽1,000 for 10; Mulch ₽100; rancher ₽2,500;
    coins ₽500 for 50.
  - Poffins read ₽3,200; there was no Poffin Case, so nothing was bought. The Soda Pop, Lemonade and 500-coin charges
    were checked in the script only.
  - Selling back at a Mart: Honey ₽50 and Moomoo Milk ₽250, the same as they cost.
  - Before the Hall of Fame, the old prices and texts are unchanged.

## CB-0124 — 2026-10-25, 1:52 am US Central (session on `claude/sweet-wright-rk4wl1`)

**Implemented: the placement tile check (`docs/LegendaryPlacementReview.md` item 9), and four fixes it found.** PR #136
(the event-script and map-link checks) is merged.
- **New tool `tools/test/check_object_tiles.py`** checks each object Sovereign added or moved (256), against vanilla's
  events in git (commit 22fddfd4):
  - It reports objects sharing a tile with another object, a sign or hidden item, a warp or a trigger.
  - It walks tile collision (bit 15 of the land data) under the engine's rules: Surf, Waterfall, Rock Climb, one-way
    ledges, bike ramps (1 or 3 tiles), no turning off an elevated bike bridge (`src/player_move.c`), counters.
  - It reports any object the player can't reach and anything an object cuts off.
  - On-purpose cases are in its `KNOWN` list with reasons.
  - It needs git history, so it runs locally, not in CI. Checking every vanilla object would need an allowlist of
    about 100 entries (Great Marsh's shared matrix, drained lakes, decorations).
- **Fixed:**
  - **Zygarde** couldn't be reached: (21, 4) is under Wayward Cave B1F's east-west bike bridge. It's now in the

    his rematch's) and Breanna's Wormadam are affected. Stats are now recalculated after the form.

  frames to get past Torment, Disable and an empty PP; A handles messages and doubles targets.

## CB-0140 — 2026-10-25, 5:05 pm US Central (session on `claude/sweet-wright-rk4wl1`)

**Implemented: the Elite Four run test, and a fix for double battles with one Pokémon.** PR #151 (gift test part 2)
is merged.
- `tools/test/elite_four_runs.py` plays the Elite Four and Cynthia on each path through `docs/EliteFourFormat.md`, for
  13 runs of five battles:
  - the six first-challenge paths;
  - the rematch menu's three entries with the rematch teams, and all doubles before Stark Mountain;
  - three runs with one Pokémon, where the warning before a double battle is answered yes or no.
- Each battle must have the right trainer (singles or Sovereign doubles, first or rematch team) and format, and must
  be won. The heaps must be intact after each battle (`tools/test/heap_check.py`, new).
- **Result: all 13 runs pass, after a game fix.** Before the fix, the 10 full-party runs passed and the 3 one-Pokémon
  runs crashed or wrote over memory in the room after a double battle.
- **The bug (since CB-0032, Totem trials too):**
  - The empty second slot had no sprite data. Move animations load each battler's palette (`BattleMonOBJData_New`),
    so the empty slot's came from NARC 0, member 0, which isn't a palette.
  - The failed load was freed as `Heap_Free(NULL)`, which broke the system heap's free list.
  - Found with heap walks between frames, then a hook on `Heap_Free(NULL)`.
- **The fix:** `BattleDisplay_SovereignSetAbsentBattler` gives the slot a real palette (species 0's) and blank tiles,
  as a fainted battler's slot keeps. Lone and full-party Totem trials leave the heaps intact too.
- **Test-build hooks:**
  - `gSovereignTestTrainerBattles`: each trainer battle's trainer, battle type and result;
  - `gSovereignTestVar` takes four vars or flags (a flag below `VARS_START`: 0 clears it, anything else sets it).
- The hooks don't change the shipping ROM: with the fix, it builds to the same bytes with and without them
  (sha1 `cffefd95…`). It builds without warnings and passes the boot smoke test.

## CB-0141 — 2026-10-25, 7:42 pm US Central (session on `claude/sweet-wright-rk4wl1`)

**Implemented: the Trainers' School Egg in the gift test, heap checks in every playtest harness, and the fix for
harness workers dying after 250 cases.** PR #152 (Elite Four runs and the lone-Pokémon heap fix) is merged.
- **The Trainers' School Egg:** `tools/test/gift_pokemon.py` now covers 59 runs. The Cowgirl's Egg must be one of the
  18 babies her script picks from, at Lv. 1, with its species' moves. The party hook notes `isEgg`, so no other gift
  may be an Egg. **All 59 pass** (this run's Egg: Wynaut).
- **Heap checks everywhere:** a broken heap only shows later, so the harnesses check the heaps (`heap_check.py`) at
  each point where the field is free again, and report **HEAP**:
  - `battle_all_trainers.fight()`, shared by the trainer, species, form-change, evolution and Egg tests;
  - `talk_to_objects.play_out` and `gift_pokemon.talk_and_answer`, shared by the object, sign and trigger, gift and
    Elite Four tests;
  - `visit_changed_maps.py`, on each map.
- **Fix: the workers that died after 250 cases.** Each `savestate.load_file` reopens desmume's SDL sound, which takes
  one more hold on SDL's audio subsystem and on its events subsystem (`SDL_InitSubSystem(SDL_INIT_AUDIO)` with no
  quit; found with an `LD_PRELOAD` shim). At 255 holds SDL 2.0.22 asserts and the process exits (code 42).
  `Driver.load_state` in `tools/test/drive.py` calls `SDL_QuitSubSystem(SDL_INIT_AUDIO | SDL_INIT_EVENTS)` after
  each load, and all eight harnesses that load per case use it. A process now loads 400 states with no abort.
- **Results with the checks, all with no HEAP:** gifts 59/59, species 1,510/1,510, form changes 211/211, signs 84/84,
  objects 252/252 (142 ran, 110 nothing), maps 182 (174 idle, 8 scenes as before).
- **Found in the object test (harness, not game):** it counts any script as the object's.
  - Route 212's night policemen (trainer scripts 3088 and 3089) "ran" for Stake 1 and Grunt 3, which are hidden on a
    new game.
  - An A press during the warp can talk to the object early. At Snowpoint that fights the temple guards, so Aran is
    removed before the test's own A press.
  - The test clock is the host's.
- The shipping ROM is unchanged (sha1 `cffefd95…`): only test hooks and tools changed.

## CB-0142 — 2026-10-25, 9:28 pm US Central (session on `claude/sweet-wright-rk4wl1`)

**Implemented: the object test checks it ran the object's own script.** PR #153 (the School Egg, heap checks, the SDL

- **The fix:** `tools/test/talk_to_objects.py`'s `talk()` now does the following.
  - After the A press, the first script the field started (`gSovereignTestScripts`) must be the object's own:
    `script_id()` gives its script, or for a trainer 3000/5000 + ID − 1 (jsoncnv `from_script`).
  - Another script first (a trainer who sees the player): it plays out, the test warps back, and it talks once more.
  - **STANDIN** (passes): another object answered from the object's tile. That is an object placed on the same tile,
    or any object of the map the second time.
  - **OTHER** (fails): a different script twice in a row.
- **Warps:** `play_out(wait_for_script=True)` presses nothing until a script starts. Before this, an A press meant for
  a scene could talk to the object as the warp ended: Snowpoint's temple guards were fought early and Aran removed.
- **Clock:** `arrive()` sets `gSovereignTestClock` to noon (`HOUR`) for the object, sign, gift and wild-form tests.
  Route 212's policemen are out at night and had answered for hidden objects.

## CA-0058 (Claude A, 2026-10-26): separate front and back palettes

**For Claude B:** battle sprites' backs now have palettes of their own. `pl_pokegra.narc` gains two back palettes per
species after the existing files (`make_pl_pokegra.py`), and `BuildPokemonSpriteTemplate` loads them for
`FACE_BACK` (`POKEGRA_BACK_PALETTE_BASE + species * 2 + shiny`). #1–493 get copies of their front palettes. If a
harness reads palettes from `pl_pokegra` by index, front indices are unchanged.

#494–1025 reconverted (7 species still reduced, from 165). Release build, boot smoke test and an archive slot check
pass.
